=== idx: 932 ===
Gold supporting: transcription - coupled repair ( tcr ) is generally observed as more rapid or more efficient removal of certain types of dna damage from the transcribed strands of expressed genes compared with the nontranscribed strands.
LLM supporting:  the context provided does not include a specific sentence that directly addresses the gene strand targeted by transcription - coupled repair ( tcr ). however, based on the general understanding of tcr and its role in nucleotide excision repair ( ner ), tcr primarily targets the non - template ( or coding ) strand of dna. this is because tcr is activated when a transcriptionally active rna polymerase encounters a lesion in the dna template, leading to a block in transcription. tcr then removes the lesion from the non - template strand, allowing transcription to resume. this mechanism is crucial for maintaining the integrity of the genetic code, as it ensures that the correct sequence is transcribed into mrna, which is then translated into protein. the involvement of tcr in other types of dna damage, such as those caused by oxidative stress, is still under investigation and not as well - established.
